893 entries, be-audit to beads-writing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **be-audit**: Audit code backend/tầng dữ liệu của MGST theo bộ rule đã chốt — SQL & hiệu năng (mgst-db-design.md §11), phân quyền, snapshot lịch sử, transaction, bảo mật. - **be-auth**: Use when wiring authentication into a Node.js backend. Issues JWT access + rotating refresh tokens as HttpOnly cookies, hashes passwords with argon2, mounts /auth routes, and exports a requireAuth middleware. Source of truth for the contract fe-auth consumes. ([HieuHo-hdh/claude-skills](https://github.com/HieuHo-hdh/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/be-auth) · ★1)
- **be-auth**: Backend identity and access. Covers OIDC login against any discovery-capable IdP (Auth Code + PKCE), magic-link applicant sessions, server-side principal sessions, and RBAC (role/role_permission/role_assignment/group_mapping). Also an OAuth2 authorization server that issues scoped opaque tokens for MCP agents. Use when working on login/callback/logout, /auth/me, magic-links, sessions, RBAC permission resolution, OAuth scopes/consent/grants, or bootstrap admins in backend/app/modules/auth. ([STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow](https://github.com/STUPA-MAKERS/STUPA-Workflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/be-auth) · ★1)
- **be-auth**: Authentication, authorisation and hardening for bl-backend — Supabase Auth sessions, the permission matrix from SECURITY_MODEL.md enforced in a guard on every route plus RLS underneath, object-level checks against BOLA/IDOR, webhook signatures, rate limits, and secret hygiene. Use for every endpoint; it's the checklist bl-security will review against. ([westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes](https://github.com/westmatty92-alt/northside-quotes/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/be-auth))
- **be-auth-and-security**: Web application security for backends and frontends - authentication (sessions vs JWT, refresh rotation, third-party identity providers), authorization and object-level checks, password handling, CSRF, CORS, XSS, SQL injection, SSRF, file upload risks, secure headers, rate limiting, secrets, dependency hygiene, and an OWASP-aligned checklist. Use when adding login, roles, tokens, uploads, public endpoints, or before launch. ([Khawarizm-Academy/Khawarizm-Academy](https://github.com/Khawarizm-Academy/Khawarizm-Academy/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/be-auth-and-security) · ★2)

- **be-avatars**: Gravatar proxy for person avatars — GET /api/principals/{id}/avatar and /api/principals/me/avatar fetch the Gravatar image (SHA-256 of the e-mail, d=404) from the fixed host gravatar.com through the webhook SSRF guard, cache hit and miss in Redis, and answer the bytes with ETag/Cache-Control or a problem+json 404.
